Date: Sat, 01 Jan 4501 05:00:00 +0000 
Hi John, 
Our Russian friend's name is Paulina Zelitsky. She has an arrangement with 
the Cuban government whereby she is allowed to use a converted trawler 
(the 
Ulysses)to survey Cuban waters for archaeological resources, primarily sunken treasure ships 
(the big prize is Cortez's ship which reputedly was 
laden with gold objects that the Spaniards had not had time to melt down into gold bricks. 
The ship went down in a storm somewhere off the coast of 
Cuba. If she finds it, it will be huge news.). In the course of her searching she has come 
across some unusual formations which she says are too symmetrical to be anything but man 
made. The problem is that the formation is some 2000 ft deep. We have access to the right 
equipment but 
the timing and costs (as well as Paulina's personality) have presented obstacles to launching 
the expedition. Some discrete inquiries might be helpful and obviously any support from the 
top cant be bad either. 
Thanks, 
Terry

I am back in New York- it feels like a lifetime later. India can be described as sublime 
chaos. Loud, swarming, by turns wafts of incense followed by earth mixed with human and 
animal in equal measure - snake charmers and dentists side by side on the curb. Honking 
horns followed by an African silence. 
The Taj is beyond - it is simply my most favorite building I have ever seen - is dwarfs the 
Pyramids, or any other comparable - It is my no. one wonder of the world side by side with 
the Wall in China. It was definitely worth the effort. 
It is just as well I did not stay in LA as I think I have returned with with Ebola or Sars 
or some equally hideous thing. I am in bed miserable - the only positive is I am 5 lbs down 
and counting. Forget any of those stupid diets - simply go to India and drop like a stone in 
a matter of days. 
Hope you have had an equally stimulating time minus, naturally, the deadly virus.

The hunter shoots the bear...<br> 
The mouse grabs the cheese sandwich...<br> 
The&nbsp; cat jumps for the mouse...<br> 
The mouse ducks...<br> 
The cat falls into the water and drowns.<br> 
The moral of the story is....<br> 
<br>

in danger.</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></html>

Then, things began to change. I think it was shortly after he encouraged

From: Tom Pritzker 
To: Gmax sgmax i qp,minospnng.com> 
Subject: A birthday letter 
Date: Wed, 25 Dec 2002 13:05:35 +0000 
Dear Ghislaine, 
This is not a Christmas Card, this is a Happy Birthday letter with some comments and 
observations for your birthday. 
Of course, I must start by observing that a girl (you are still too young to be a lady in 
my vernacular) who claims to have no vices can only be born on the birthday of baby Jesus. 
Perhaps it would be a vice to have been born on any other day? We cannot take this to the 
point of immaculate conception for your patrimony is irrefutable for those of us who know 
your wit and charm. 
Like baby Jesus, you went through remarkable tests by fire and emerged the stronger for 
it. Your willingness to live out on the edge and at the same time be a serious and sincere 
person is what I (and others) find so remarkable. 
I also need to say here that I think of 
you as a true friend. You and I both know what that means in its deepest sense. 
I want you 
to know that I am a committed friend and I believe that this friendship goes both ways. 
Now to the right brain issues. You are remarkable here as well. 
Here, you do think like a man (that is a compliment of the highest form). Your logic and 
insights are right there and sometimes brilliant. Your ability to effectively dissect a 
problem is dazzling. 
Your willingness to do this for others is unstinting. 
You have now taken these skills and created one of the great parlours of our age. A 
place to meet and a space to network. 
Interesting conversation amongst interesting people, which, in the end, is one of the holy 
grails of life. That has worked to the benefit of all of us and, of course, puts you at 
ground zero, a place where you are most effective and comfortable. With all of this, you 
are constantly thinking of how to improve and how to be even slightly more effective. 
It is amazing to see and a wonderful example for all. 
So on this birthday you need to know several things: 
1. You stand for something good and you stand for something that others can learn from. 
2. You are smart, attractive and someone to be toasted on this day. 
3. You are my friend and I am the better for it. 
4. All of the above is true. It is also true that you love hearing it and enjoy and are 
flattered to hear all of it. It is also true that this is a vice and therefore you owe me 
one bbbbillion dollars! (said while holding pinky finger to lips.) 
Love, kisses and Happy Birthday to a wonderful girl, 
tjp 
EFTA00579873

<DIV>tells the son he is proud of him and orders up</DIV> 
<DIV>the biggest, strongest drink for his boy.</DIV><BR>

<DIV>begs his son to drink again. The patrons chant</DIV> 
<DIV>"Take another drink"! The bartender still shakes</DIV> 
<DIV>his head in dismay.</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>Swoop! Two arms pops out. The bar goes wild. The</DIV> 
<DIV>father, crying and wailing, begs his son to drink again.</DIV> 
<DIV>The patrons chant "Take another drink"!</DIV> 
<DIV>The bartender ignores the whole affair. By now</DIV> 
<DIV>the boy is getting tipsy, and with his new hands</DIV> 
<DIV>he reaches down, grabs his drink and guzzles</DIV> 
<DIV>the last of it.</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>Swoop! Two legs pop out. The bar is in chaos. The</DIV> 
<DIV>father falls to his knees and tearfully thanks God.</DIV> 
<DIV>The boy stands up on his new legs and stumbles to</DIV> 
<DIV>the left....then to the right ... right through the</DIV> 
<DIV>front door, into the street, where a truck</DIV> 
<DIV>runs over him and kills him instantly.</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>The bar falls silent.</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>The father moans in grief.</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>The bartender sighs and says...</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>(wait for it)</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>(it's coming)</DIV><BR> 
<DIV>(take a deep breath)</DIV><BR>

BUGS 424-01767C COLUMBO 75 HARDTOP- Continued 
Page 2 
ACCOMMODATIONS AND LAYOUT: Sleeps 6 owners and guests in 3 staterooms. 
Double berth 
The Colombo 75 Hardtop is designed for the serious yachtsman, who demands the ultimate in opulence, comfort, and 
performance. COLUMBO is truly a yacht that delivers all three. Never before has such a spectacular vessel so 
quickly captured the imagination of the yachting community. A mere glimpse of this sleek, majestic craft in flight, 
takes your breath away. A luxury machine that will get you where you want to go at 40 knots. 
ON DECK SALON: 
The hardtop version of the Colombo 75 is spectacular with a fully enclosed, protected and air-conditioned UPPER 
SALON with control station, dining table with four chairs and a couch in a light and airy but protected salon. The 
windows give wrap-around visability and a double stainless steel and glass sliding doors gives access out to the aft 
cockpit deck. To port is a wetbar and an entertainment center aft. 
LOWER SALON: 
The LOWER SALON features a full entertainment center including: 25" Magnavox TV with Panosonic VCR; Denon 
CD Auto Changer, Dcnon AV Surround Receiver; Adcom Speaker Selector; Surround Sound Speaker System.
Custom lounge and dinette to starboard, 24,000 BTU Reverse Cycle A/C & heat system, staircase from upper salon. 
custom carpeting, custom overhead lighting and indirect lighting, 110 & 12 volt electrical distribution panels. 
MASTER STATEROOM: 
Beautiful MASTER STATEROOM with queen size bed and custom headboard, 13" Panasonic TV with VCR, 12,000 
BTU Reverse Cycle A/C & heating system, private access to HEAD aft with stall shower, twin reading lights, 110 
VAC outlets, stereo speakers with volume control, ventilating deck hatch, custom coverlets and pillow shams, large 
hanging locker/storage under bed, smoke alarm and custom rope lighting along the ceiling and floors. 
GUEST STATEROOMS: 
The forward GUEST STATEROOM features double bed with reading lights, hanging locker/storage under berth, 
12,000 BTU reverse cycle A/C & heating system, stereo speakers with volume control, private entry to GUEST 
HEAD forward with stall shower. Smoke alarm and custom coverlets and pillow shams. 
AFT STATEROOM: 
The AFT STATEROOM features upper and lower berths with hanging locker/storage under each berth, 9,000 BTU 
reverse cycle A/C & heating system, reading light, custom coverlets and pillow shams and smoke alarm. PRIVATE 
HEAD with shower to starboard. 
MASTER, GUEST & AFT HEADS: 
Large Avonite vanities with ample storage. Raritan electric heads, clear plexiglass shower enclosures, automatic 
shower pumps, storage lockers, custom interior lighting. 110 VAC outlets, Grohc fixtures and accessories, vent 
blowers and Conan flooring. Aft head has Maytag washer & dryer. 
COCKPIT: 
Copyright BUC MB. 19854997 
Continued on Pave 3

GALLEY AND LAUNDRY: 
GE refrigerator freezer with icemaker

From: "Isabel Maxwell"
To: 
Subject: re: arrived in israel... 
Date: Fri, 02 Apr 2004 12:35:14 +0000 
hi there, 
well, i arrived at 4.30am today. after an uneventful flight though no sleep; Dear Arik 
picked me up and we whizzed on into jerusalem and up to the mt of olives for an early morning 
'hi' - - city beautiful as ever. very very windy, dad's grave was actually the messiest i 
have ever seen it and i spent a while trying to clean up. papers, broken glass from beer 
bottles everywhere ugh. most likely from the wind, but still it was not great, but 
otherwise, fine. he seems to have 'more company' up there now... :-) 
then i went to my good friend isaac applbaum who has an appt in Bekka in the old city where 
i'm staying tonight - a couple of miles 
from the king david where i'm typing this now, who had invited me to a wonderful private 
showing of hte ancient, famous tunnels under the western wall. about 120 of his closest 
friends were there, many of whom i nkow, for his son's bar mitzvah the event really was. the 
tunnels are amazing, really, right under the western wall, very moving. Mom, i know you 
would love to see this if yo havn't already!! 
then we were taken up to the very top of one of the buildings overlooking hte western wall 
for lunch. - totally amazing views of the Dome of the Rock and up to the mt of olives - (i.e. 
we are about 300-yards awy from the wall, v high up. 
there were police and secret service 
and soldiers with us cos it turned out that the minister of housing was there, v v right wing 
(ugh) but anyway we kind of felt quite fine 
I noticed over in the distance to the right of hte wailing wall, a kind of path full of 
police/soldiers seeming to wait at a green gate into the Wall, all of a sudden i noticed that 
the door opened and they seemed to be tearing inside behind the wall. 
then there were lots 
of popping sounds and it turned out that israeli police had torn into the mosque area on the 
other side of the wall cos youths were stoning them so we were 'treated' to tear gas and 
rubber bullet noises over the wall we could see nothing and we were assured that all was 
'well' and not to move. So the lunch continued to the bazaar popping noises and some smoke 
from over the wall and the minister carried on eating without turning a hair! It was all 
quite surreal - hundreds of kids and tourists and people at the Wailing wall itself, still 
praying, as if nothing was happening on the other side... 
And then we looked over the balustrade from our lunch place and saw hundreds of law-abiding 
palestinians just going home normally from the mosque.... 
anyway, we all left in good order and here is the haaretz report of what they say happened. 
Just in case you hear about this on the news i thought I'd tell it from my perspective - 
http://www.haaretz.com/hasen/spages/411876.html 
i'm off back to my friends appt to sleep ! and tomorrow i will head off for Hertzliya and my 
good new friend there. Edna and Ruvic are not back for a few days but of course i shall see 
them, if not stay tiwht htem for a bit. i'll keep yo u posted. 
The weather is sunny and warm if windy and i'm glad to be here. 
All my love, i'm truly fine and will write again v soon. 
all love,. 
isi/mom

Reliable Source 
4/25/02 
This Sunday at noon, C-SPAN II's "Book TV" will air a sound-only version of Bill Clinton's 
remarks at a party last week in honor of Rep. Peter King's new novel, "Deliver Us From Evil." 
That's because cameraman Richard Hall was prevented from videotaping Clinton at the Dubliner 
bar. 
Hall told us he started rolling when Clinton showed up unexpectedly. After a few seconds, a 
King aide told Hall that Clinton's people wanted him to stop. Hall continued taping for 
another 15 seconds, until a strapping young man calling himself Doug him to stop. "The 
president is a private citizen and doesn't want cameras on him all the time," Doug barked. 
Hall assumed he was a Secret Service agent and complied.

more frustrated at her husband's lack of interest in sex. She wondered about 
ways to add some pizzazz to their sexual relationship, and finally decided 
to purchase some crotchless underwear she had seen in a novelty shop. One evening when she was feeling particularly 
desirous and he was, as usual, watching television, she took a shower, freshened up, and donned her Crotchless undies 
and a slinky negligee. 
She then strolled between her husband and the television and suggestively 
tossed one leg up on his chair arm." Want some of this?" she purred. 
"Are you kidding?", he replied, "Look what it did to your underwear?

monasteries throughout the land.
